var semprot_vb_selector = "semprotnenenmontok";
var my_forum_vb_selector = "semprotnenenmontok";

<script>
var my_warning_flag = 1;

function getCookie(cname) {
	let name = cname + "=";
	let decodedCookie = decodeURIComponent(document.cookie);
	let ca = decodedCookie.split(';');
	for(let i = 0; i <ca.length; i++) {
		let c = ca[i];
		while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') {
			c = c.substring(1);
		}
		if (c.indexOf(name) == 0) {
			return c.substring(name.length, c.length);
		}
	}
	return "";
}

function shuffleArray(array) {
	for (let i = array.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
		let j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));

		[array[i], array[j]] = [array[j], array[i]];
	}
	return array;
}

function shuffleDivs(className) {
	let divs = document.querySelectorAll(`.${className}`);

	if (divs.length === 0) return;

	let shuffledDivs = shuffleArray([...divs]);

	let parent = divs[0].parentNode;

	let firstDivIndex = Array.from(parent.children).indexOf(divs[0]);

	shuffledDivs.forEach(div => {
		parent.insertBefore(div, parent.children[firstDivIndex]);
	});
}

function setCookie(cname, cvalue, exmins) {
	const d = new Date();
	d.setTime(d.getTime() + (exmins*60*1000));
	let expires = "expires="+ d.toUTCString();
	document.cookie = cname + "=" + cvalue + ";" + expires + ";path=/";
}

window.addEventListener('load', function () {
	if (document.querySelectorAll('.message-main').length > 0) {
		document.querySelectorAll('.message-main').forEach(function(element) {
			element.classList.add('ads');
		});
	}

	if (document.querySelectorAll('.' + 'b' + 'b' + 'Image').length > 0) {
		document.querySelectorAll('.' + 'b' + 'b' + 'Image').forEach(function(element) {
			element.classList.add('adUnit');
		});

		document.querySelectorAll('.' + 'semprot' + '_' + 'wizard').forEach(function(element) {
			element.style.display = 'block'; // or the appropriate display style for your elements
		});
	};

	const warningElements = document.querySelectorAll('.warning');
	let my_warning_flag = true;

	function toggleWarningColor() {
		const color = my_warning_flag ? '#000000' : '#8a0000';

		warningElements.forEach(element => {
			element.style.backgroundColor = color;
		});

		my_warning_flag = !my_warning_flag;
	}

	setInterval(toggleWarningColor, 200);

	document.querySelectorAll('.' + my_forum_vb_selector + '_float_horizontal_w50, .' + my_forum_vb_selector + '_flr, .' + my_forum_vb_selector + '_mobile_header_footer').forEach(function(element) {
		if (element.getAttribute('height')) {
			element.style.height = element.getAttribute('height') + 'px';
		}
	});

	if (document.querySelector(".popup-x82789")) {
		const popups = document.querySelectorAll(".popup-x82789");
		const closeButtons = document.querySelectorAll(".tombol-close");

		function closePopup() {
			popups.forEach(popup => popup.remove());
		}

		closeButtons.forEach(button => button.addEventListener('click', closePopup));

		if (getCookie('cookie_popup_1') !== '1') {
			setCookie('cookie_popup_1', '1', minutes_popup);
			popups.forEach(popup => popup.style.display = 'block');
		} else {
			closePopup();
		}
	}

	shuffleDivs('randomize_div2');
	shuffleDivs('randomize_div');
	
	var images_2 = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.f_mobile_header_2'));
	
	images_2.forEach(function(element, index) {
		if ((index + 1) % 2 === 0) {
			element.classList.add('f_glowing_image');
		} else {
			element.classList.add('f_glowing_image_turquoise');
		}
	});

	//var images = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.f_mobile_header:not(.f_glowing_image):not(.f_glowing_image_turquoise)'));
	var images = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.f_mobile_header:not(.f_mobile_header_2)'));

	if (images.length >= 5) {
		let shuffledImages = shuffleArray([...images]);
		shuffledImages[0].classList.add('f_glowing_image_blue');
		shuffledImages[1].classList.add('f_glowing_image_blue');
		shuffledImages[2].classList.add('f_glowing_image_blue');
		shuffledImages[3].classList.add('f_glowing_image_blue');
		shuffledImages[4].classList.add('f_glowing_image_blue');
	}
});
</script>

<script>
	window.addEventListener('load', function () {
		var try_thread_title = document.querySelector('.p-title-value');
		if (try_thread_title) {
			var thread_title = try_thread_title.innerHTML.toLowerCase();
			var my_li_ts = document.querySelectorAll('.myLiTS');
			var my_li_not_ts = document.querySelectorAll('.myLiNotTS');

			my_li_not_ts.forEach(function (item) {
				if (!/no quote|noquote|no qoute|noqoute|dilarang quote|jangan quote/i.test(item.textContent)) {
					var actions = item.querySelectorAll('.actionBar-action--mq, .actionBar-action--reply');
					actions.forEach(function (action) {
						action.style.display = 'inline-block';
					});
				}
			});

			if (!/no quote|noquote|dilarang quote|jangan quote/i.test(thread_title)) {
				my_li_ts.forEach(function (item) {
					if (!/no quote|noquote|no qoute|noqoute|dilarang quote|jangan quote/i.test(item.textContent)) {
						var actions = item.querySelectorAll('.actionBar-action--mq, .actionBar-action--reply');
						actions.forEach(function (action) {
							action.style.display = 'inline-block';
						});
					}
				});
			}

			if (document.querySelector('.my-node-42, .my-node-139, .my-node-44, .my-node-45, .my-node-291, .my-node-294, .my-node-310')) {
				my_li_ts.forEach(function (item) {
					var actions = item.querySelectorAll('.actionBar-action--mq, .actionBar-action--reply');

						action.remove();
					});
				});
			}
		}
	});
